```/Core/vitest.setup.ts diff
-/** The dialer a suite installs on the window: its own channel table, dispatched the way the preload dispatches the real one. An unstubbed channel answers undefined rather than throwing. */
+/** The dialer a suite installs on the window: its own channel table, dispatched the way the preload dispatches the real one. An unstubbed `ask` answers a failure `Result` the way a refusing handler would, so a reader of `r.ok` takes its failure path. */
 export function stubDialer(channels: Record<string, unknown>): Dialer {
   const call = (k: string, ...args: unknown[]): unknown =>
     (channels[k] as ((...a: unknown[]) => unknown) | undefined)?.(...args)
   return {
-    ask: (k: string, ...args: unknown[]) => Promise.resolve(call(k, ...args)),
+    ask: (k: string, ...args: unknown[]) =>
+      Promise.resolve(k in channels ? call(k, ...args) : fail('not-found', `${k} is not stubbed.`)),
     tell: call,
     on: (k: string, cb: unknown) => (call(k, cb) as (() => void) | undefined) ?? (() => {}),
   } as unknown as Dialer
```

##### /Core/Navigation/previewRules.ts

**Delta:** +51(+51/-0)

```/Core/Navigation/previewRules.ts diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+// The rules judge what the tree already holds, so routing a click costs no read.
+
+import { contextIdsOf } from '../Contexts/contextIdentity'
+import { entityMemo, type NexusTree } from '../Nexus/tree'
+import {
+  contextPaneTargets,
+  LOCATION_TARGET,
+  type PaneTarget,
+  STAMP_TARGETS,
+  TITLE_TARGET,
+} from '../Properties/Cells/PropertyTypes'
+import { RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID } from '../Properties/properties'
+import { containerAt } from '../Nexus/treePatch'
+import { relDirname } from '../Paths/posix'
+import { settingOf } from '../Settings/personalization'
+import { filtering } from '../Views/filterRules'
+import { prepareGroup } from '../Views/Pipeline/filter'
+import { toRow } from '../Views/Pipeline/group'
+import { pageValuesOf } from '../Views/pageValues'
+
+const previewTestOf = entityMemo(
+  [(t) => t.config.personalization.openInPreview, (t) => t.contexts],
+  (tree) => {
+    const rules = settingOf(tree.config.personalization, 'openInPreview')
+    return filtering(rules)
+      ? prepareGroup(rules.rules, [], tree.collections, contextIdsOf(tree))
+      : undefined
+  },
+)
+
+/** What the rules can judge from the tree alone, so Last Modified and registry properties stay out. */
+export const previewTargets = (tree: NexusTree): PaneTarget[] => [
+  TITLE_TARGET,
+  ...(tree.collections.length > 0 ? [LOCATION_TARGET] : []),
+  ...STAMP_TARGETS.filter((t) => t.id === RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID.createdAt),
+  ...contextPaneTargets(tree),
+]
+
+export function opensInPreview(
+  tree: NexusTree,
+  { id, path }: { id: string; path: string },
+): boolean {
+  const test = previewTestOf(tree)
+  if (!test) return false
+  const folder = containerAt(tree, relDirname(path))
+  const page = folder?.pages.find((p) => p.id === id)
+  if (!folder || !page) return false
+  return test(
+    toRow(page, folder.id, { [id]: pageValuesOf(id, {}, null) }, tree.config.pageMetadata),
+  )
+}
```

```/Core/Settings/ExcludedDirectoriesRow.tsx diff
+            (next) => rename(folder, next),
+            () => void commit(stored.filter((f) => f !== folder)),
+            folder,
+          )}
+        </Reveal>
+      ))}
+      {drafting ? (
+        <Reveal open enterOnMount fill key="draft">
+          {fieldRow(
+            '',
+            (n) => void commitDraft(n),
+            () => setDrafting(false),
+            'draft',
+          )}
+        </Reveal>
+      ) : null}
+      <FootingCreate
+        ariaLabel="Add exclusion"
+        disabled={drafting || busy}
+        onClick={() => setDrafting(true)}
+      />
+    </div>
```

```/Core/Settings/ManageRow.tsx diff
+import { type CSSProperties, type ReactNode, useRef, useState } from 'react'
+import { Button } from '@pommora/uix/Buttons/Button'
+import { PickerMenu } from '@pommora/uix/Pickers/PickerMenu'
+import { SettingsFieldRow } from './SettingsFieldRow'
+import * as x from './settings-rows.css'
+
+/** A settings row whose value is a list edited in its own pane: the count, and Manage to open it. */
+export function ManageRow({
+  label,
+  hint,
+  count,
+  style,
+  children,
+}: {
+  label: string
+  hint?: string
+  count: number
+  style?: CSSProperties
+  children: ReactNode
+}): React.JSX.Element {
+  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
+  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
+  return (
+    <SettingsFieldRow label={label} hint={hint}>
+      <span className={x.manageCluster}>
+        <span className={x.count}>{count}</span>
+        <Button
+          ref={triggerRef}
+          type="filled"
+          label="Manage"
+          pressed={open}
+          onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
+        />
+        <PickerMenu
+          open={open}
+          onDismiss={() => setOpen(false)}
+          triggerRef={triggerRef}
+          bareSurface
+          contentClassName={x.managePane}
+          style={style}
+        >
+          {children}
+        </PickerMenu>
+      </span>
+    </SettingsFieldRow>
+  )
+}
```

```/Core/Settings/PreviewRulesRow.tsx diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+import { PANE_MIN_W } from '@pommora/uix/Menus/frame-slide.css'
+import { personalizationOf } from '../Session/configSlice'
+import { useSession, useSetting } from '../Session/store'
+import { previewTargets } from '../Navigation/previewRules'
+import { FilterFrame, filterViewOf, standalonePatch } from '../Views/Settings/FilterFrame'
+import { ManageRow } from './ManageRow'
+import { settingOf } from './personalization'
+
+const live = () => settingOf(personalizationOf(useSession.getState()), 'openInPreview')
+
+export function PreviewRulesRow({
+  label,
+  hint,
+}: {
+  label: string
+  hint?: string
+}): React.JSX.Element | null {
+  const value = useSetting('openInPreview')
+  const tree = useSession((s) => s.tree)
+  const setPersonalization = useSession((s) => s.setPersonalization)
+  if (!tree) return null
+  return (
+    <ManageRow
+      label={label}
+      hint={hint}
+      count={value.rules?.rules.length ?? 0}
+      style={{ minWidth: PANE_MIN_W }}
+    >
+      <FilterFrame
+        locations={tree.collections}
+        view={filterViewOf(value)}
+        read={() => filterViewOf(live())}
+        schema={[]}
+        tree={tree}
+        targets={previewTargets(tree)}
+        onCommit={(p) => setPersonalization('openInPreview', { ...live(), ...standalonePatch(p) })}
+      />
+    </ManageRow>
+  )
+}
```

##### /Core/Views/filterRules.ts

**Delta:** +93(+93/-0)

```/Core/Views/filterRules.ts diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+// The filter-rule schema, its operators and helpers, and the standalone filter.
+
+import { z } from 'zod'
+import { isPlainObject } from '../Contract/validators'
+import { eachOf, idArray } from '../Files/decoders'
+
+const MATCH_MODES = ['all', 'any'] as const
+export type MatchMode = (typeof MATCH_MODES)[number]
+
+const filterRule = z.object({
+  property_id: z.string(),
+  op: z.string(),
+  value: z.string().optional(),
+  values: idArray.optional(),
+})
+export type FilterRule = z.infer<typeof filterRule>
+
+/** RECURSIVE: a child may itself be a FilterGroup, expressing mixed AND/OR. Whether the filter APPLIES is a separate axis (a view's `filter_enabled`, a standalone filter's `enabled`), so turning it off never costs it its authored mode. */
+export type FilterGroup = { match: MatchMode; rules: Array<FilterRule | FilterGroup> }
+
+// A rule this build can't read drops alone, and the rest still filter.
+export const filterGroup: z.ZodType<FilterGroup> = z.object({
+  match: z.enum(MATCH_MODES),
+  get rules() {
+    return eachOf(z.union([filterRule, filterGroup]))
+  },
+})
+
+export const FILTER_OPS = {
+  is: 'is',
+  isNot: 'is_not',
+  contains: 'contains',
+  doesNotContain: 'does_not_contain',
+  isEmpty: 'is_empty',
+  isNotEmpty: 'is_not_empty',
+  greaterThan: 'greater_than',
+  lessThan: 'less_than',
+  onOrAfter: 'on_or_after',
+  onOrBefore: 'on_or_before',
+  endsOnOrAfter: 'ends_on_or_after',
+  endsOnOrBefore: 'ends_on_or_before',
+  startsWith: 'starts_with',
+  containsAll: 'contains_all',
+  containsAny: 'contains_any',
+  isBefore: 'is_before',
+  isAfter: 'is_after',
+  greaterOrEqual: 'greater_or_equal',
+  lessOrEqual: 'less_or_equal',
+  isInside: 'is_inside',
+  isNotInside: 'is_not_inside',
+} as const
+
+/** The ops that are complete without an operand; everything else is unauthored until one arrives. */
+export const OPERANDLESS_OPS = new Set<string>([FILTER_OPS.isEmpty, FILTER_OPS.isNotEmpty])
+
+export const SUBSTRING_OPS = new Set<string>([
+  FILTER_OPS.contains,
+  FILTER_OPS.doesNotContain,
+  FILTER_OPS.startsWith,
+])
+
+/** A chip list wins over a single value, as the pane writes one or the other. */
+export const ruleOperands = (rule: FilterRule): string[] =>
+  rule.values?.length ? rule.values : rule.value != null ? [rule.value] : []
+
+export const answerable = (rule: FilterRule): boolean =>
+  OPERANDLESS_OPS.has(rule.op) || ruleOperands(rule).length > 0
+
+export const isGroup = (node: FilterRule | FilterGroup): node is FilterGroup =>
+  isPlainObject(node) && Array.isArray((node as { rules?: unknown }).rules)
+
+export function mapRules(
+  group: FilterGroup,
+  fn: (rule: FilterRule) => FilterRule | null,
+): FilterGroup {
+  return {
+    ...group,
+    rules: group.rules.flatMap<FilterRule | FilterGroup>((node) =>
+      isGroup(node) ? [mapRules(node, fn)] : isPlainObject(node) ? (fn(node) ?? []) : [node],
+    ),
+  }
+}
+
+/** A filter that isn't a view's: its rules and whether they apply travel together. */
+export const standaloneFilter = z.object({
+  rules: filterGroup.nullable().catch(null),
+  enabled: z.boolean().catch(true),
+})
+export type StandaloneFilter = z.infer<typeof standaloneFilter>
+export const NO_FILTER: StandaloneFilter = { rules: null, enabled: true }
+
+export const filtering = (f: StandaloneFilter): f is { rules: FilterGroup; enabled: true } =>
+  f.enabled && f.rules !== null
```

```/Core/Views/pageValues.ts diff
+import { localIso } from '@pommora/uix/Utilities/pad'
+import { ID_KEY } from '../Nexus/identityMark'
+import { idTime } from '../Nexus/ids'
+import { pageFrontmatter } from '../Nexus/schemas'
+import type { PageValues } from './viewRow'
+
+export function pageValuesOf(
+  id: string,
+  fm: Record<string, unknown>,
+  mtimeMs: number | null,
+): PageValues {
+  return {
+    frontmatter: pageFrontmatter.parse({ ...fm, [ID_KEY]: id }),
+    createdAt: localIso(idTime(id)),
+    modifiedAt: localIso(mtimeMs),
+  }
```

```/Core/Views/Pipeline/creationSeeds.ts diff
+export function filterSeeds({
+  filter,
+  blank,
+  schema,
+  setTree,
+  contextIds,
+}: {
+  filter: FilterGroup | undefined
+  blank: ViewRow
+  schema: PropertyDefinition[]
+  setTree: SetTreeNode[]
+  contextIds: readonly string[]
+}): Seeds {
+  if (!filter) return {}

+  const judge = (
+    node: FilterRule | FilterGroup,
+  ): { seeds: Seeds; met: boolean; passes?: boolean } => {
+    if (!isGroup(node)) {
+      const passes = prepareRule(node, schema, setTree, contextIds)?.(blank)
+      const seed = ruleSeed(node, schema, contextIds)

+        seeds: seed ? { [node.property_id]: seed } : {},
+        met: passes !== false || seed !== null,
+        passes,
+      }
+    }
+    const passes = prepareGroup(node, schema, setTree, contextIds)?.(blank)
+    const children = node.rules.map(judge)
+    switch (node.match) {
+      case 'all':
+        return {
+          seeds: children.reduce<Seeds>((seeds, c) => mergeSeeds(seeds, c.seeds), {}),
+          met: children.every((c) => c.met),
+          passes,
+        }
+      case 'any': {
```

##### /Core/Views/Settings/LocationField.tsx

**Delta:** +208(+208/-0)

```/Core/Views/Settings/LocationField.tsx diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
+import type { CollectionNode, SetNode } from '../../Nexus/tree'
+import { Icon } from '@pommora/uix/Symbols'
+import { TokenField } from '@pommora/uix/Fields/TokenField'
+import { foldKey, matchScore } from '../../Paths/caseFold'
+import { EntityIcon } from '../../Assets/EntityIcon'
+import { DisclosureRow, MenuScrollFrame, pickRuns, useDisclosureSet } from '@pommora/uix/Menus'
+import { PickerMenu } from '@pommora/uix/Pickers/PickerMenu'
+import { PICKER_MAX_HEIGHT, treePane } from '@pommora/uix/Pickers/picker-base.css'
+import { useKeepInView, usePaneCtl } from '@pommora/uix/Interactions/paneCtl'
+import { toggled } from '@pommora/uix/Utilities/checkSet'
+import * as fp from './filter-frame.css'
+import { emptyPicker, useMultiValue } from './valueField'
+
+export type Folder = CollectionNode | SetNode
+
+export function flattenNodes(nodes: Folder[] | undefined): Folder[] {
+  return (nodes ?? []).flatMap((n) => [n, ...flattenNodes(n.sets)])
+}
+
+/** Matches with their whole subtrees, under the ancestors that lead to them; `leads` names those ancestors, which open by default. */
+function narrowed(nodes: Folder[], query: string): { nodes: Folder[]; leads: Set<string> } {
+  const leads = new Set<string>()
+  const walk = <N extends Folder>(ns: N[]): N[] =>
+    ns.flatMap((n) => {
+      const sets = walk(n.sets ?? [])
+      if (sets.length > 0) leads.add(n.id)
+      if (matchScore(foldKey(n.title), query) !== null) return [n]
+      return sets.length > 0 ? [{ ...n, sets }] : []
+    })
+  return { nodes: walk(nodes), leads }
+}
+
+const shownRows = (nodes: Folder[], isOpen: (id: string) => boolean): Folder[] =>
+  nodes.flatMap((n) => [n, ...(isOpen(n.id) ? shownRows(n.sets ?? [], isOpen) : [])])
+
+function LocationTree({
+  nodes,
+  shown,
+  disclosure,
+  activeId,
+  keepInView,
+  onPick,
+}: {
+  nodes: Folder[]
+  shown: string[]
+  disclosure: { has: (id: string) => boolean; toggle: (id: string) => void }
+  activeId: string | null
+  keepInView: (el: HTMLElement | null) => void
+  onPick: (id: string) => void
+}): React.JSX.Element {
+  const renderLevel = (ns: Folder[]): React.JSX.Element[] => {
+    const runs = pickRuns(
+      ns.map((n) => shown.includes(n.id)),
+      ns.map((n) => disclosure.has(n.id) && (n.sets?.length ?? 0) > 0),
+    )
+    return ns.map((n, i) => {
+      const kids = n.sets ?? []
+      const active = n.id === activeId
+      return (
+        <DisclosureRow
+          key={n.id}
+          ref={active ? keepInView : undefined}
+          title={n.title}
+          icon={<EntityIcon kind={n.kind} icon={n.icon} size="body" />}
+          dropOutline={kids.length > 0 ? 'chevron' : 'spacer'}
+          open={disclosure.has(n.id)}
+          onToggle={() => disclosure.toggle(n.id)}
+          onClick={() => onPick(n.id)}
+          active={active}
+          picked={runs[i]}
+        >
+          {kids.length > 0 ? renderLevel(kids) : undefined}
+        </DisclosureRow>
+      )
+    })
+  }
+  return (
+    <MenuScrollFrame maxHeight={PICKER_MAX_HEIGHT}>
+      {nodes.length === 0 ? emptyPicker('No Sets in this collection.') : renderLevel(nodes)}
+    </MenuScrollFrame>
+  )
+}
+
+const NO_FLIPS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set()
+
+export function LocationField({
+  values,
+  nodes,
+  folderById,
+  names,
+  onCommit,
+}: {
+  values: string[]
+  nodes: Folder[]
+  folderById: ReadonlyMap<string, Folder>
+  names: boolean
+  onCommit: (next: string[]) => void
+}): React.JSX.Element {
+  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
+  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('')
+  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
+  const { shown, toggle } = useMultiValue(values, onCommit)
+  const expanded = useDisclosureSet()
+  const [narrow, setNarrow] = useState({ q: '', ids: NO_FLIPS })
+  const q = foldKey(draft.trim())
+  const tree = useMemo(
+    () => (q ? narrowed(nodes, q) : { nodes, leads: new Set<string>() }),
+    [nodes, q],
+  )
+  const flipped = narrow.q === q ? narrow.ids : NO_FLIPS
+  const disclosure = q
+    ? {
+        has: (id: string) => tree.leads.has(id) !== flipped.has(id),
+        toggle: (id: string) =>
+          setNarrow((prev) => ({ q, ids: toggled(prev.q === q ? prev.ids : NO_FLIPS, id) })),
+      }
+    : expanded
+  const rows = shownRows(tree.nodes, disclosure.has)
+  const pick = (id: string): void => {
+    toggle(id)
+    setDraft('')
+  }
+  const enter = (name: string): void => {
+    if (!names) return
+    const fold = foldKey(name)
+    if (!shown.some((v) => foldKey(v) === fold)) toggle(name)
+    setDraft('')
+  }
+  const dismiss = (): void => {
+    setOpen(false)
+    setDraft('')
+  }
+  const { row, ctl } = usePaneCtl(
+    rows,
+    open ? draft : null,
+    { open, pick: (n) => pick(n.id), close: dismiss },
+    null,
+  )
+  const keepInView = useKeepInView(row?.id)
+
+  return (
+    <>
+      <TokenField
+        ref={ref}
+        chrome="bordered"
+        className={fp.locationField}
+        entries={shown.map((v) => {
+          const node = folderById.get(v)
+          return {
+            key: v,
+            label: node?.title ?? v,
+            icon: node ? (
+              <EntityIcon kind={node.kind} icon={node.icon} size="body" />
+            ) : (
+              <Icon name="folder-search" size="body" />
+            ),
+            onRemove: () => toggle(v),
+          }
+        })}
+        draft={draft}
+        placeholder="Value"
+        ariaLabel="Filter values"
+        onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
+        onBlur={dismiss}
+        onPress={() => setOpen(true)}
+        onKeyDown={(e) => {
+          if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
+            e.preventDefault()
+            if (open) ctl.current.move(e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1)
+            else setOpen(true)
+          } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && open && row) {
+            e.preventDefault()
+            ctl.current.pick()
+          } else if (e.key === 'Escape' && open) {
+            // The field drives its pane's keys, so Escape closes it whether the pane is shown or hidden.
+            e.preventDefault()
+            ctl.current.close()
+          }
+        }}
+        onDraftChange={(d) => {
+          setDraft(d)
+          setOpen(true)
+        }}
+        onEnter={enter}
+      />
+      <PickerMenu
+        open={open && (!q || tree.nodes.length > 0)}
+        onDismiss={dismiss}
+        triggerRef={ref}
+        origin="left"
+        contentClassName={treePane}
+        focus="keep"
+      >
+        {open && (
+          <LocationTree
+            nodes={tree.nodes}
+            shown={shown}
+            disclosure={disclosure}
+            activeId={row?.id ?? null}
+            keepInView={keepInView}
+            onPick={pick}
+          />
+        )}
+      </PickerMenu>
+    </>
+  )
+}
```

##### /Core/Views/Settings/valueField.tsx

**Delta:** +21(+21/-0)

```/Core/Views/Settings/valueField.tsx diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+import { useState } from 'react'
+import { MenuCaption } from '@pommora/uix/Menus'
+import { sameItems } from '@pommora/uix/Utilities/same'
+import { toggleValue } from '../../Properties/Pickers/PropertyPicker'
+
+export const emptyPicker = (label: string): React.JSX.Element => <MenuCaption>{label}</MenuCaption>
+
+export function useMultiValue(
+  values: string[],
+  onCommit: (next: string[]) => void,
+): { shown: string[]; toggle: (v: string) => void } {
+  const [local, setLocal] = useState<string[] | null>(null)
+  if (local && sameItems(local, values)) setLocal(null)
+  const shown = local ?? values
+  const toggle = (v: string): void => {
+    const next = toggleValue(shown, v)
+    setLocal(next)
+    onCommit(next)
+  }
+  return { shown, toggle }
+}
```

```/UIX/Fields/TokenField.tsx diff
+import { type KeyboardEvent, type Ref, useRef } from 'react'

+import * as s from './fields.css'
+
+/** Values standing in a field beside a bare caret: Enter offers the draft, and Backspace on an empty caret removes the last value. The host owns the draft and decides what Enter makes; a key its `onKeyDown` prevents is left to it. */
+export function TokenField({
+  entries,
+  draft,
+  onDraftChange,
+  onEnter,
+  onFocus,
+  onBlur,
+  onPress,
+  onKeyDown,
+  placeholder,
+  ariaLabel,
+  chrome,
+  className,
+  ref,
+}: {
+  entries: RunEntry[]
+  draft: string
+  onDraftChange: (draft: string) => void
+  onEnter: (draft: string) => void
+  onFocus?: () => void
+  onBlur?: () => void
+  /** A press anywhere on the field but a chip's ×, which an already-focused caret reports no focus for. */
+  onPress?: () => void
+  onKeyDown?: (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void
+  placeholder?: string
+  ariaLabel?: string
+  chrome?: FieldChrome
+  className?: string
+  ref?: Ref<HTMLDivElement>
+}): React.JSX.Element {
+  const caret = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
+  return (
+    <InputField
+      ref={ref}
+      chrome={chrome}
+      className={cx(s.editable, className)}
+      onMouseDown={(e) => {
+        const target = e.target as Element
+        if (target.closest('button')) return e.preventDefault()
+        if (target !== caret.current) {
+          e.preventDefault()
+          caret.current?.focus()
+        }
+        onPress?.()
+      }}
+    >
+      {entries.length > 0 && <FieldRun entries={entries} />}
+      <input
+        ref={caret}
+        className={cx(s.base, s.tokenCaret)}
+        value={draft}
+        placeholder={entries.length > 0 ? undefined : placeholder}
+        aria-label={ariaLabel}
+        spellCheck={false}
+        onFocus={onFocus}
+        onBlur={onBlur}
+        onChange={(e) => onDraftChange(e.target.value)}
+        onKeyDown={(e) => {
+          onKeyDown?.(e)
+          if (e.defaultPrevented) return
+          if (e.key === 'Enter') {
+            e.preventDefault()
+            if (draft.trim()) onEnter(draft.trim())
+          } else if (e.key === 'Backspace' && draft === '') entries.at(-1)?.onRemove?.()
+        }}
+      />
+    </InputField>
+  )
+}
```

##### /UIX/Interactions/paneCtl.ts

**Delta:** +53(+53/-0)

```/UIX/Interactions/paneCtl.ts diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type RefObject } from 'react'
+import { clamp } from '../Utilities/clamp'
+import { useLatest } from '../Utilities/stableApi'
+
+/** The control a pane's keys drive while focus stays with its owner. */
+export interface PaneCtl {
+  open: boolean
+  pick: () => void
+  move: (d: number) => void
+  close: () => void
+  aside?: (dir: 1 | -1) => boolean
+}
+
+export function usePaneCtl<T>(
+  rows: readonly T[],
+  resetKey: unknown,
+  drive: {
+    open: boolean
+    pick: (row: T) => void
+    close: () => void
+    aside?: (dir: 1 | -1) => boolean
+  },
+  initial: number | null = 0,
+): { index: number | null; row: T | null; ctl: RefObject<PaneCtl> } {
+  const [index, setIndex] = useState<number | null>(initial)
+  const count = rows.length
+  const selected = index === null ? null : Math.min(index, Math.max(count - 1, 0))
+
+  const ctl = useLatest<PaneCtl>({
+    open: drive.open,
+    pick: () => {
+      const row = rows[selected ?? 0]
+      if (row !== undefined) drive.pick(row)
+    },
+    move: (d) =>
+      setIndex((i) =>
+        i === null ? (d > 0 ? 0 : count - 1) : clamp(Math.min(i, count - 1) + d, 0, count - 1),
+      ),
+    close: drive.close,
+    aside: drive.aside,
+  })
+
+  useEffect(() => setIndex(initial), [resetKey, initial])
+
+  return { index: selected, row: selected === null ? null : (rows[selected] ?? null), ctl }
+}
+
+export function useKeepInView(active: unknown): (el: HTMLElement | null) => void {
+  return useCallback(
+    (el: HTMLElement | null) => el?.scrollIntoView({ inline: 'nearest', block: 'nearest' }),
+    [active],
+  )
+}
```

```/UIX/Utilities/pad.ts diff
@@ -4 +4 @@ export const pad = (n: number, width = 2): string => String(n).padStart(width, '
 // Never toISOString: a UTC key shifts the day west of Greenwich; formatters read date-only as LOCAL midnight.
 export const localDayKey = (d: Date): string =>
   `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`
+
+// Local-clock form, the same shape the date picker writes.
+export function localIso(ms: number | null): string | null {
+  if (ms === null) return null
+  const d = new Date(ms)
+  return `${localDayKey(d)}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`
+}
```

```/Core/Navigation/previewRules.test.ts diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
+import { idAt } from '../Nexus/ids'
+import type { NexusTree } from '../Nexus/tree'
+import { makeTree } from '../Testing/testTree'
+import type { FilterRule, StandaloneFilter } from '../Views/filterRules'
+import { opensInPreview } from './previewRules'
+
+const JUNE_1 = new Date(2026, 5, 1, 9).getTime()
+const top = { id: idAt(JUNE_1), path: 'Notes/Alpha.md' }
+const deep = { id: idAt(JUNE_1 - 30 * 86_400_000), path: 'Notes/Ideas/Drafts/Deep.md' }
+
+function treeWith(openInPreview?: StandaloneFilter): NexusTree {
+  const base = makeTree({ personalization: { openInPreview } })
+  return {
+    ...base,
+    collections: [
+      {
+        kind: 'collection',
+        id: 'c1',
+        title: 'Notes',
+        path: 'Notes',
+        pages: [{ kind: 'page', title: 'Alpha Plan', contextValues: { g1: ['a1'] }, ...top }],
+        sets: [
+          {
+            kind: 'set',
+            id: 's1',
+            title: 'Ideas',
+            path: 'Notes/Ideas',
+            pages: [],
+            sets: [
+              {
+                kind: 'set',
+                id: 's2',
+                title: 'Drafts',
+                path: 'Notes/Ideas/Drafts',
+                pages: [{ kind: 'page', title: 'Deep', ...deep }],
+                sets: [],
+              },
+            ],
+          },
+        ],
+      },
+    ],
+  }
+}
+
+const any = (...rules: FilterRule[]): StandaloneFilter => ({
+  rules: { match: 'any', rules },
+  enabled: true,
+})
+const judge = (filter: StandaloneFilter | undefined, page: { id: string; path: string }) =>
+  opensInPreview(treeWith(filter), page)
+
+describe('opensInPreview', () => {
+  const title: FilterRule = { property_id: '_title', op: 'contains', value: 'alpha' }
+
+  it('opens nothing without rules, with rules off, or with an emptied group', () => {
+    expect(judge(undefined, top)).toBe(false)
+    expect(judge({ ...any(title), enabled: false }, top)).toBe(false)
+    expect(judge({ rules: { match: 'any', rules: [] }, enabled: true }, top)).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('folds case on a Title rule', () => {
+    expect(judge(any(title), top)).toBe(true)
+    expect(judge(any(title), deep)).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('reads Location Is as the page’s own Collection', () => {
+    const rule = { property_id: '_location', op: 'is', values: ['c1'] }
+    expect(judge(any(rule), top)).toBe(true)
+    expect(judge(any(rule), deep)).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('reads Location Contains through every level beneath a Set', () => {
+    const rule = { property_id: '_location', op: 'is_inside', values: ['s1'] }
+    expect(judge(any(rule), deep)).toBe(true)
+    expect(judge(any(rule), top)).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('reads a typed folder name, and a name no folder holds matches nothing', () => {
+    expect(judge(any({ property_id: '_location', op: 'is_inside', values: ['ideas'] }), deep)).toBe(
+      true,
+    )
+    expect(judge(any({ property_id: '_location', op: 'is_inside', values: ['Gone'] }), deep)).toBe(
+      false,
+    )
+  })
+
+  it('reads a Context Is Any from the tree’s own context values', () => {
+    const rule = { property_id: 'g1', op: 'contains_any', values: ['a1', 't1'] }
+    expect(judge(any(rule), top)).toBe(true)
+    expect(judge(any(rule), deep)).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('reads Created from the page’s id', () => {
+    const rule = { property_id: '_created_at', op: 'on_or_after', value: '2026-05-15' }
+    expect(judge(any(rule), top)).toBe(true)
+    expect(judge(any(rule), deep)).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('rejudges the same folders when the rules or the Contexts change', () => {
+    const context = { property_id: 'g1', op: 'contains_any', values: ['a1'] }
+    const on = treeWith(any(title, context))
+    const withRules = (openInPreview: StandaloneFilter): NexusTree => ({
+      ...on,
+      config: { ...on.config, personalization: { openInPreview } },
+    })
+    expect(opensInPreview(on, top)).toBe(true)
+    expect(opensInPreview(withRules({ ...any(title), enabled: false }), top)).toBe(false)
+    const byContext = withRules(any(context))
+    expect(opensInPreview(byContext, top)).toBe(true)
+    expect(opensInPreview({ ...byContext, contexts: [] }, top)).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('opens nothing for a path that no longer holds the page', () => {
+    expect(judge(any(title), { ...top, path: 'Notes/Ideas/Alpha.md' })).toBe(false)
+  })
+})
```

```/Core/Session/openTarget.test.tsx diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+// @vitest-environment jsdom
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { act, createElement } from 'react'
+import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client'
+import { makeTree } from '../Testing/testTree'
+import { useNavData } from '../Navigation/useNavData'
+import { stubDialer } from '../vitest.setup'
+import { useConnections } from './pageConnections'
+import { useSession } from './store'
+;(globalThis as { IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true
+
+const alpha = { kind: 'page', id: 'p1', path: 'Notes/Alpha.md' } as const
+const beta = { kind: 'page', id: 'p2', path: 'Notes/Ideas/Beta.md' } as const
+const set = { kind: 'set', id: 's1', path: 'Notes/Ideas' } as const
+
+const matchingAlpha = makeTree({
+  personalization: {
+    openInPreview: {
+      rules: { match: 'any', rules: [{ property_id: '_title', op: 'contains', value: 'alpha' }] },
+      enabled: true,
+    },
+  },
+})
+
+const { select: realSelect, openWindowTab: realOpenWindowTab } = useSession.getState()
+
+let root: Root | null = null
+afterEach(() => {
+  const r = root
+  root = null
+  if (r) act(() => r.unmount())
+  useSession.setState({ select: realSelect, openWindowTab: realOpenWindowTab })
+})
+
+async function probe<T>(read: () => T): Promise<() => T> {
+  let latest: T
+  const Probe = (): null => {
+    latest = read()
+    return null
+  }
+  root = createRoot(document.createElement('div'))
+  await act(async () => root?.render(createElement(Probe)))
+  return () => latest
+}
+
+describe('openTarget', () => {
+  const select = vi.fn(async () => {})
+  const openWindowTab = vi.fn()
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    select.mockClear()
+    openWindowTab.mockClear()
+    useSession.setState({ tree: matchingAlpha, select, openWindowTab })
+  })
+
+  it('sends ⌘ to a new tab, even for a page the rules match', () => {
+    expect(useSession.getState().openTarget(alpha, { newTab: true })).toBe('tab')
+    expect(select).toHaveBeenCalledWith(alpha, { newTab: true, heading: undefined })
+    expect(openWindowTab).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('sends a page the rules match to the Page Window at its heading', () => {
+    expect(useSession.getState().openTarget(alpha, { heading: 'Setup' })).toBe('window')
+    expect(openWindowTab).toHaveBeenCalledWith(alpha, { heading: 'Setup' })
+    expect(select).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('leaves a page the rules miss, and anything but a page, to Default Opening Behavior', () => {
+    expect(useSession.getState().openTarget(beta, { heading: 'Setup' })).toBe('tab')
+    expect(select).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(beta, { newTab: undefined, heading: 'Setup' })
+    expect(useSession.getState().openTarget(set)).toBe('tab')
+    expect(select).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(set, { newTab: undefined, heading: undefined })
+    expect(openWindowTab).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('previews a connection the rules match while Open Connections In Preview is off', async () => {
+    const bundle = await probe(() => useConnections('preview'))
+    bundle()?.open({ id: alpha.id, title: 'Alpha', path: alpha.path }, 'Setup')
+    expect(openWindowTab).toHaveBeenCalledWith(alpha, { heading: 'Setup' })
+    expect(select).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+})
+
+describe('a Navigation row', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    ;(window as unknown as { nexus: unknown }).nexus = stubDialer({})
+    useSession.setState({ tree: matchingAlpha, windowSlot: null, windowsFile: { sets: {} } })
+  })
+
+  it('keeps the NavWindow open when it opens a page the rules match into it', async () => {
+    const nav = await probe(useNavData)
+    useSession.getState().openNav()
+    act(() => nav().go({ kind: 'page', id: alpha.id }, () => useSession.getState().closeWindow()))
+    const slot = useSession.getState().windowSlot
+    expect(slot?.kind).toBe('nav')
+    expect(slot?.tabs.map((t) => t.target)).toContainEqual(alpha)
+  })
+
+  it('closes the NavWindow when it opens a page the rules miss into a tab', async () => {
+    const nav = await probe(useNavData)
+    useSession.getState().openNav()
+    act(() => nav().go({ kind: 'page', id: beta.id }, () => useSession.getState().closeWindow()))
+    expect(useSession.getState().windowSlot).toBeNull()
+  })
+})
```

```/Core/Settings/PreviewRulesRow.test.tsx diff
+// @vitest-environment jsdom
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { act } from 'react'
+import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client'
+import { PreviewRulesRow } from './PreviewRulesRow'
+import { useSession } from '../Session/store'
+import { stubDialer } from '../vitest.setup'
+import { makeTree } from '../Testing/testTree'
+import type { FilterGroup, FilterRule, StandaloneFilter } from '../Views/filterRules'
+import { RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID } from '../Properties/properties'
+import { RESERVED_LABEL } from '../Properties/Cells/columnLabel'
+import { count } from './settings-rows.css'
+
+;(globalThis as { IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true
+
+let host: HTMLDivElement
+let root: Root
+
+const titleRule: FilterRule = {
+  property_id: RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID.title,
+  op: 'contains',
+  value: 'a',
+}
+const createdRule: FilterRule = { property_id: RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID.createdAt, op: 'is_empty' }
+
+const render = async (openInPreview?: StandaloneFilter): Promise<void> => {
+  ;(window as unknown as { nexus: unknown }).nexus = stubDialer({
+    'personalization:set': vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true, value: null })),
+  })
+  useSession.setState({
+    tree: makeTree({
+      personalization: openInPreview ? { openInPreview } : {},
+      registry: [{ id: 'prop_mood', name: 'Mood', type: 'text' }],
+    }),
+  })
+  host = document.createElement('div')
+  document.body.appendChild(host)
+  root = createRoot(host)
+  await act(async () =>
+    root.render(
+      <PreviewRulesRow
+        label="Open In Preview"
+        hint="Pages matching these rules open within preview windows by default."
+      />,
+    ),
+  )
+}
+
+afterEach(async () => {
+  await act(async () => root.unmount())
+  document.body.innerHTML = ''
+})
+
+const stored = (): unknown => useSession.getState().tree?.config.personalization.openInPreview
+const click = async (el: Element | undefined): Promise<void> => {
+  await act(async () => (el as HTMLElement).click())
+}
+const byLabel = (label: string): HTMLElement[] =>
+  Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(`[aria-label="${label}"]`))
+const manage = (): HTMLButtonElement =>
+  Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).find(
+    (b) => b.textContent === 'Manage',
+  ) as HTMLButtonElement
+const offered = (): (string | null)[] =>
+  [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-picker-portal] *')].map((el) => el.textContent)
+const option = (text: string): Element | undefined =>
+  [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-picker-portal] *')]
+    .filter((el) => el.textContent === text)
+    .at(-1)
+
+describe('PreviewRulesRow', () => {
+  it('the count reads the rule count', async () => {
+    await render({ rules: { match: 'any', rules: [titleRule, createdRule] }, enabled: true })
+    expect(host.getElementsByClassName(count)[0]?.textContent).toBe('2')
+  })
+
+  it('adding a rule through the pane writes the key', async () => {
+    await render()
+    await click(manage())
+    await click(byLabel('Filter property')[0])
+    await click(option(RESERVED_LABEL[RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID.createdAt]))
+    expect(stored()).toEqual({
+      rules: {
+        match: 'any',
+        rules: [expect.objectContaining({ property_id: RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID.createdAt })],
+      },
+      enabled: true,
+    })
+  })
+
+  it('turning the footer Off keeps the rules and writes enabled: false', async () => {
+    const rules: FilterGroup = { match: 'any', rules: [titleRule] }
+    await render({ rules, enabled: true })
+    await click(manage())
+    await click(byLabel('Filter active')[0])
+    expect(stored()).toEqual({ rules, enabled: false })
+  })
+
+  it('clearing the last rule with the filter on stores no key', async () => {
+    await render({ rules: { match: 'any', rules: [titleRule] }, enabled: true })
+    await click(manage())
+    await click(byLabel('Remove filter')[0])
+    expect(stored()).toBeUndefined()
+  })
+
+  it('offers Title, Location, Created, and each Context, and neither Last Modified nor a user property', async () => {
+    await render()
+    await click(manage())
+    await click(byLabel('Filter property')[0])
+    const labels = offered()
+    for (const t of ['Title', 'Location', RESERVED_LABEL[RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID.createdAt], 'Realms'])
+      expect(labels).toContain(t)
+    expect(labels).not.toContain(RESERVED_LABEL[RESERVED_PROPERTY_ID.modifiedAt])
+    expect(labels).not.toContain('Mood')
+  })
+})
```

```/Core/Views/handlers.test.ts diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
+import { readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises'
+import { tempRoot } from '../Testing/hostFs'
+import type { HostContext } from '../Contract/handlers'
+import { NEEDS_CONFIG_PATCH } from '../Contract/validators'
+import { createFolderEntity } from '../Nexus/folderEntity'
+import { newId } from '../Nexus/ids'
+import { dropLiveTree, refreshTree } from '../Nexus/liveTree'
+import { closeSession, openSession } from '../Nexus/session'
+import { sidecarPath } from '../Paths/paths'
+import { viewsHandlers } from './handlers'
+
+const ctx = { push: () => {} } as unknown as HostContext
+
+let root: string
+let sidecar: string
+
+beforeEach(async () => {
+  root = tempRoot('pom-views-handlers-')
+  const c = await createFolderEntity(root, 'collection', 'Notes', newId())
+  if (!c.ok) throw new Error('setup failed')
+  sidecar = sidecarPath(c.value.path, 'collection')
+  await openSession(root)
+  await refreshTree(root)
+})
+
+afterEach(async () => {
+  closeSession()
+  dropLiveTree()
+  await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
+})
+
+describe('container:configure', () => {
+  const configure = (patch: unknown) =>
+    viewsHandlers['container:configure'](ctx, 'Notes', 'collection', patch)
+
+  it('writes a valid view_button', async () => {
+    expect((await configure({ view_button: 'labeled' })).ok).toBe(true)
+    expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(sidecar, 'utf8')).view_button).toBe('labeled')
+  })
+
+  it.each([
+    [{ view_button: 'huge' }],
+    [{}],
+  ])('refuses %j and leaves the sidecar as it was', async (patch) => {
+    const before = await readFile(sidecar, 'utf8')
+    expect(await configure(patch)).toEqual(NEEDS_CONFIG_PATCH)
+    expect(await readFile(sidecar, 'utf8')).toBe(before)
+  })
+})
```

```/Core/Views/Host/useViewHost.test.tsx diff
+      mutate: vi.fn(async (req: { id: string }) => ({
+        created: { path: `Col/${req.id}.md` },
+      })) as never,
+    })
+    await mount({
+      ...collection({
+        ...SORTED,
+        filter: {
+          match: 'any',
+          rules: [
+            { property_id: 'prop_pri', op: 'is', value: 'High' },
+            { property_id: 'prop_status', op: 'is', value: 'complete' },
+          ],
+        },
+      }),
+      properties: [statusDef, priorityDef],
+    } as CollectionNode)
+    const p1 = api?.rowById.get('p1')
+    await act(async () => void creation.createAdjacent(p1!, 'below'))
+    const mutate = useSession.getState().mutate as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>
+    expect(mutate.mock.calls[0][0].seeds).toEqual({
+      prop_status: { kind: 'select', value: 'complete' },
+    })
+  })
+})
+
 describe('a create placed ahead of its reply', () => {
   const complete = propsAtRoot({ prop_status: 'complete' }, [threeStatus])
   const commits: { band?: string; order: string[] }[] = []
```

```/Core/Views/Settings/FilterFrame.test.tsx diff
+const locationField = (): HTMLInputElement =>
+  host.querySelector('input[aria-label="Filter values"]') as HTMLInputElement
+const focusLocation = async (): Promise<void> => {
+  await act(async () => {
+    locationField().focus()
+  })
+}
+const typeLocation = async (text: string): Promise<void> => {
+  // React reads the value off its own tracker, so a bare assignment looks like no change at all.
+  const setValue = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLInputElement.prototype, 'value')?.set
+  await act(async () => {
+    setValue?.call(locationField(), text)
+    locationField().dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }))
+  })
+}
+const pressEnter = async (): Promise<void> => {
+  await act(async () => {
+    locationField().dispatchEvent(
+      new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', bubbles: true, cancelable: true }),
+    )
+  })
+}
+const pressKey = async (key: string): Promise<void> => {
+  await act(async () => {
+    locationField().dispatchEvent(
+      new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }),
+    )
+  })
+}
+const blurLocation = async (): Promise<void> => {
+  await act(async () => {
+    locationField().blur()
+  })
+}
+const pressLocation = async (): Promise<void> => {
+  await act(async () => {
+    locationField().dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }))
+  })
+}
+const pickerRows = (): string[] =>
+  [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-picker-portal] [role="button"]')].map(
+    (el) => el.textContent ?? '',
+  )
+

+    sets,

+  })
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    Object.assign(source, {
+      sets: [

+      ],
+    })
+  })
+  afterEach(() => {
+    Object.assign(source, { sets: [] })
+  })
+  const locationRule = (op: string, values?: string[]): SavedView =>
+    view({
+      filter: {
+        match: 'any',
+        rules: [{ property_id: '_location', op, ...(values ? { values } : {}) }],
+      },
+    })
+  const savedValues = (): unknown =>
+    ((lastSaved().filter as { rules: unknown[] }).rules[0] as Record<string, unknown>).values
+
+  it('focusing the field opens the picker unfiltered', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is_inside'))
+    await focusLocation()
+    expect(pickerRows()).toEqual(['Projects', 'Archive'])
+  })
+
+  it('typing narrows the picker to matching folders with their ancestors open', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is_inside'))
+    await focusLocation()
+    await typeLocation('note')
+    expect(pickerRows()).toEqual(['Projects', 'Notes'])
+  })
+
+  it('Enter under Contains adds the typed name and clears the draft, even one naming a folder', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is_inside'))
+    await focusLocation()
+    await typeLocation('Docs')
+    await pressEnter()
+    expect(savedValues()).toEqual(['Docs'])
+    expect(locationField().value).toBe('')
+  })
+
+  it('Enter under Is adds nothing', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is'))
+    await focusLocation()
+    await typeLocation('Docs')
+    await pressEnter()
+    expect(saveSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('a name equal by fold to a shown one is not added twice', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is_inside', ['Docs']))
+    await focusLocation()
+    await typeLocation('docs')
+    await pressEnter()
+    expect(saveSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('a picked folder joins the values and clears the draft', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is_inside', ['Docs']))

+    expect(savedValues()).toEqual(['Docs', 'd1'])
+    expect(locationField().value).toBe('')
+  })
+
+  it('pressing the field opens the picker', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is_inside'))
+    await pressLocation()
+    expect(pickerRows()).toEqual(['Projects', 'Archive'])
+  })
+
+  it('Escape and blur each close the picker and clear the draft', async () => {
+    await mount(locationRule('is_inside'))
+    await focusLocation()
+    await typeLocation('doc')
+    await pressKey('Escape')
+    await settle()
+    expect(pickerRows()).toEqual([])
+    expect(locationField().value).toBe('')
+    await typeLocation('doc')

+

+

+
```

```/UIX/Fields/TokenField.test.tsx diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+// @vitest-environment jsdom
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { act } from 'react'
+import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client'
+import { TokenField } from './TokenField'
+import type { RunEntry } from './FieldRun'
+;(globalThis as { IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true
+
+let host: HTMLDivElement
+let root: Root
+beforeEach(() => {
+  host = document.createElement('div')
+  document.body.appendChild(host)
+  root = createRoot(host)
+})
+afterEach(() => {
+  act(() => root.unmount())
+  host.remove()
+})
+
+const caret = (): HTMLInputElement => host.querySelector('input') as HTMLInputElement
+
+const render = (
+  props: Partial<React.ComponentProps<typeof TokenField>> & { draft?: string } = {},
+): { onDraftChange: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>; onEnter: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> } => {
+  const onDraftChange = vi.fn()
+  const onEnter = vi.fn()
+  act(() =>
+    root.render(
+      <TokenField
+        entries={[]}
+        draft=""
+        onDraftChange={onDraftChange}
+        onEnter={onEnter}
+        placeholder="Value"
+        {...props}
+      />,
+    ),
+  )
+  return { onDraftChange, onEnter }
+}
+
+const press = (key: string): void =>
+  act(() => {
+    caret().dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }))
+  })
+
+const mousedown = (el: Element): boolean => {
+  const e = new MouseEvent('mousedown', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true })
+  act(() => {
+    el.dispatchEvent(e)
+  })
+  return e.defaultPrevented
+}
+
+const entry = (key: string, onRemove = vi.fn()): RunEntry => ({ key, label: key, onRemove })
+
+describe('TokenField', () => {
+  it('reports the draft as typed', () => {
+    const { onDraftChange } = render()
+    // React reads the value off its own tracker, so a bare assignment looks like no change at all.
+    const setValue = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLInputElement.prototype, 'value')?.set
+    act(() => {
+      setValue?.call(caret(), 'Docs')
+      caret().dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }))
+    })
+    expect(onDraftChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Docs')
+  })
+
+  it('Enter offers the trimmed draft, and nothing when it is blank', () => {
+    const { onEnter } = render({ draft: '  Docs ' })
+    press('Enter')
+    expect(onEnter).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Docs')
+    const blank = render({ draft: '   ' })
+    press('Enter')
+    expect(blank.onEnter).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('Backspace on an empty caret removes the last entry; on a draft it only edits', () => {
+    const first = vi.fn()
+    const last = vi.fn()
+    render({ entries: [entry('a', first), entry('b', last)] })
+    press('Backspace')
+    expect(last).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
+    expect(first).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+    last.mockClear()
+    render({ entries: [entry('a', first), entry('b', last)], draft: 'x' })
+    press('Backspace')
+    expect(last).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('a press past the caret focuses it, and a press on a ×, focused or not, leaves focus where it is', () => {
+    render({ entries: [entry('a')] })
+    const remove = host.querySelector('button') as HTMLElement
+    expect(mousedown(remove)).toBe(true)
+    expect(document.activeElement).not.toBe(caret())
+    expect(mousedown(host.firstElementChild as HTMLElement)).toBe(true)
+    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(caret())
+    expect(mousedown(remove)).toBe(true)
+    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(caret())
+    expect(mousedown(caret())).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it("reports a press anywhere on the field but a chip's × as a press", () => {
+    const onPress = vi.fn()
+    render({ entries: [entry('a')], onPress })
+    mousedown(host.firstElementChild as HTMLElement)
+    mousedown(caret())
+    mousedown(host.querySelector('button') as HTMLElement)
+    expect(onPress).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
+  })
+
+  it('leaves a key the host handler prevented alone', () => {
+    const { onEnter } = render({ draft: 'Docs', onKeyDown: (e) => e.preventDefault() })
+    press('Enter')
+    expect(onEnter).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  })
+
+  it('shows the placeholder only while there are no entries', () => {
+    render()
+    expect(caret().getAttribute('placeholder')).toBe('Value')
```

```/UIX/Interactions/paneCtl.test.tsx diff
@@ -0 +1 @@
+// @vitest-environment jsdom
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
+import { act, type RefObject } from 'react'
+import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client'
+import { type PaneCtl, usePaneCtl } from './paneCtl'
+;(globalThis as { IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true
+
+let root: Root | null = null
+afterEach(() => {
+  act(() => root?.unmount())
+  root = null
+})
+
+function mount(initial: string[]) {
+  const picked: string[] = []
+  const seen: { row: string | null; ctl: RefObject<PaneCtl> | null } = { row: null, ctl: null }
+  function Harness({ rows }: { rows: string[] }): null {
+    const { row, ctl } = usePaneCtl(rows, 'q', {
+      open: true,
+      pick: (r) => picked.push(r),
+      close: () => {},
+    })
+    seen.row = row
+    seen.ctl = ctl
+    return null
+  }
+  root = createRoot(document.createElement('div'))
+  const render = (rows: string[]) => act(() => root?.render(<Harness rows={rows} />))
+  render(initial)
+  const ctl = () => seen.ctl?.current as PaneCtl
+  return {
+    picked,
+    seen,
+    render,
+    move: (d: number) => act(() => ctl().move(d)),
+    pick: () => ctl().pick(),
+  }
+}
+
+describe('usePaneCtl', () => {
+  it('picks the highlighted row itself', () => {
+    const pane = mount(['a', 'b', 'c'])
+    pane.move(1)
+    pane.pick()
+    expect(pane.seen.row).toBe('b')
+    expect(pane.picked).toEqual(['b'])
+  })
+
+  it('steps from the clamped highlight once the list shrinks under it', () => {
+    const pane = mount(['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e'])
+    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) pane.move(1)
+    pane.render(['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'])
+    expect(pane.seen.row).toBe('d')
+    pane.move(-1)
+    expect(pane.seen.row).toBe('c')
+  })
+
+  it('picks nothing from an empty list', () => {
+    const pane = mount([])
+    pane.pick()
+    expect(pane.seen.row).toBeNull()
+    expect(pane.picked).toEqual([])
+  })
+})
```

```/.claude/Features/ContextsPM.md diff
 - **Renames** are journaled and cascade the title across every context-bearing root — each page's frontmatter and each Space's sidecar — with the registry committed last, so a crash replays forward on the next open, holding its record while a file the replay reads can't be read, and a failed commit reverses the cascade, its record holding what the reversal couldn't reach. ##### /.claude/Features/InterfacePM.md
```

```/.claude/Features/InterfacePM.md diff
@@ -65 +65 @@ The floating page window (`Core/Interface/Windows/PageWindow.tsx`): a movable, r
 
 The window is a small tabbed app of its own, and a tab holds a Page or a Space. A Page tab is the editable embed; a Space tab is its title over its tile board, fully interactive, with Rename, Edit Icon, and Show or Hide Icon on the title and its Properties panel and Change Color reached by promoting it. A connection clicked inside it, and every page one of a Space's tiles opens, appends a tab rather than navigating away, deduplicating against one already open for that entity; closing the active tab falls to its left neighbor, and closing the last closes the window. A single-tab window shows the entity's ancestry as a centered NavTrail; a second tab collapses that into a standard tab strip, and closing back to one returns it. Tabs drag-reorder and move to and from the main bar, and a tab's right-click offers Open In New Tab, the page send rows on a Page tab, and Close. The window keeps one tab set, per machine, restored against the live tree: closing the window keeps it, and closing its last tab leaves it empty. The most recent Page tabs, up to the **Active Tab Cache**, stay mounted while another shows, so a switch returns each exactly as it was left; a tab beyond the cache returns with its editor state, undo, and scroll. The incoming tab slides in from the side of the one it replaces on the main pane's tab motion. **Show Banners In Windowed Pages** and **Show Banners In Windowed Spaces** give a tab its banner, running edge to edge under the strip and any open pane at one height with the renameable title over it, and put that banner's own rows in the tab's right-click menu.  ### Toolbar Tabs
```
 
```/.claude/Features/NavigationPM.md diff
 The tab bar holds the open working set, each tab **warm** — it keeps its own scroll and editor undo, so flipping back lands where you left off. The most recently visited tabs of every kind go further, as many as the **Active Tab Cache** setting (5–25, default 5): their view is parked off screen, fully built, rather than torn down, so a banner, a board, or an editor shows on return exactly as it was left, and an embedded website survives a tab flip with its session — its media paused on the switch when **Pause Media On Tab Switch** is on, and not resumed on return. A plain click from any of these but a breadcrumb segment opens a page the **Open In Preview** rules match in the Page Window instead.
 - **Persistence** — closing Pommora never resets the tabs; they reopen cold on relaunch, each machine keeping its own set. Warm state is session-only.
 - **Lifecycle** — closing the active tab focuses its left neighbor, while a tab that closes on its own hands focus to the most recently used one; the close × shows only on unpinned tabs; a deleted entity's unpinned tab closes, or follows the page that now sits at its path, while its pinned tab hides, the pin staying stored. The last tab closing drops to NavView, and opening an entity already in a tab focuses that tab.
 - **Interaction** — within-zone drag reorders, pinned among pinned and unpinned among unpinned; a page or Space tab tugged off its row moves between the main bar and a floating window's strip at the pointed slot, and a gallery card, list row, sidebar row, Table row, page card, or Set card dropped on either row opens its page, Space, or Set there — a Set on the main row only — in each case without activating the seated tab, and a carried item brings a hidden tab bar or window strip into view while it's held; Ctrl+Tab cycles all tabs, or the Page Window's or NavWindow's tabs while it holds focus; a tab's right-click offers Pin or Unpin and Close, with Preview above them where the tab holds a page, a Space, or the Matrix and the page send rows between; a pinned tab, carrying no label, leads with Open. **Reveal Tab Bar On Hover** hides the bar when idle.
```

##### /.claude/Features/NexusSyncPM.md

**Delta:** ±0(+1/-1)

```/.claude/Features/NexusSyncPM.md diff
@@ -60 +60 @@ A landing pushes an unpushed edit first, and captures the local bytes, under the
 
 A landed file is written atomically under the writer's modification time and records no write echo, leaving the receiving watcher to treat it as an ordinary external change. A landed rename moves one file and its base record, since a folder rename travels as one rename per file; a delete removes the file and an emptied parent directory. A name is held to the rules of the device that creates it, so a path another platform allowed can reach a device that can't hold it: that device passes the change over and advances its cursor, a rename onto such a name takes the page off it, and the page stays on the hub and every device that can hold it.
 
-Two kinds of file land through a merge rather than a replacement. Pommora's own JSON files — the configuration under `.nexus/`, the metadata month files, the sidecars, and the tile documents — merge key by key against the last synced bytes: a key changed on one side takes that side, a key changed on both takes the newer writer's, and the merged bytes land under the current time. Some keys hold sections that merge a level deeper, key by key within them; a metadata month file merges per page and per field, so one device clearing a page's entry and another setting a field on it keeps that field; the property registry merges per definition and per field, so a type respelled on one device and a rename made on another both survive. One of these files that doesn't parse stays on its device until it does, and one arriving that way leaves a local copy that parses in place, capturing the arrival, so the local copy ships over it.
+Two kinds of file land through a merge rather than a replacement. Pommora's own JSON files — the configuration under `.nexus/`, the metadata month files, the sidecars, and the tile documents — merge key by key against the last synced bytes: a key changed on one side takes that side, a key changed on both takes the newer writer's, and the merged bytes land under the current time. Some keys hold sections that merge a level deeper, key by key within them; `settings.json` merges per setting and a level deeper within each, so the Open In Preview switch and its rules merge apart, as the Matrix filter's do; a metadata month file merges per page and per field, so one device clearing a page's entry and another setting a field on it keeps that field; the property registry merges per definition and per field, so a type respelled on one device and a rename made on another both survive. One of these files that doesn't parse stays on its device until it does, and one arriving that way leaves a local copy that parses in place, capturing the arrival, so the local copy ships over it.
 
 A page open in any editor absorbs its landing instead of reloading under the writer: the renderer merges the landed file into the text its open editors share, and each of them takes the difference as a changes-only transaction outside undo, so every caret keeps its position; a page no editor holds reloads, capturing any text its last acknowledged save does not hold. A body write is compare-and-swap, keyed to the hash the editor last loaded or saved, and a refusal routes to the same merge or reload rather than to a retry.
```
